Once the campaign ends, we run a final view verification check and release the funds to your Vyro wallet's available balance, which is ready to cash out. You can withdraw once you've earned a minimum of $10, once per week. Withdrawals are processed via Stripe or PayPal depending on your location."}},{"@type":"Question","name":"Why was my post rejected?","acceptedAnswer":{"@type":"Answer","text":"Posts are reviewed against the rules listed in the campaign brief and platform standards. The most common reasons are not following the campaign brief, missing the required ad disclosure, or low quality and spammy content. If your post is flagged for fraudulent or botted views, your account may be permanently banned from Vyro. If you think your post was rejected in error, you can reach out to our support team."}}]}</script><section class="hero-gradient relative w-full overflow-clip rounded-3xl bg-clip-padding"><div md:py-28!"><h2 class="text-content-primary mb-8 text-center text-[32px] leading-tight font-bold tracking-tight md:mb-16 md:text-[42px] lg:text-[51px]">Frequently Asked</h2><div class="w-full divide-y divide-black/10" data-orientation="vertical"><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_pslnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">How do campaigns work on Vyro?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_4pslnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_pslnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Brands come to Vyro and create campaigns to distribute their content. Creators follow the brand&#x27;s brief to make short-form videos and post them to your own social accounts. Brands pay for the views your content generates. The more your content performs, the more you earn.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_19slnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">How much can I earn?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_59slnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_19slnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Each campaign has a pay rate listed per thousand views (CPM). Once your post is approved and meets all requirements, you&#x27;ll start earning at that rate until the campaign budget runs out. For example, if your post gains 100,000 views for a campaign with a $1 CPM, you earn $100.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_1pslnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">How do I start?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_5pslnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_1pslnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Sign up for free, connect your social accounts, and browse open campaigns. Pick a campaign, follow the brief, post your clip, and submit the link through Vyro.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_29slnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">How do I submit a clip?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_69slnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_29slnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Once you&#x27;ve posted a clip to social media, head back to Vyro and find the campaign you&#x27;re submitting to. Paste the link to your social media post and that&#x27;s it! You&#x27;ll get an email when your content has been reviewed and approved. You&#x27;ll have to link your social account to Vyro before you submit.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_2pslnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">When does my post start earning?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_6pslnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_2pslnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Views start counting as soon as you submit your post on Vyro - even before it&#x27;s reviewed. Once your post is approved and reaches the minimum view threshold, you&#x27;ll start earning. Your view count and pending earnings refresh hourly, and you earn at the campaign&#x27;s CPM rate for every eligible view while the budget is still available. Earning stops when the campaign&#x27;s budget runs out.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_39slnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">How do payouts work?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_79slnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_39slnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">While a campaign is active, your earnings are listed as &#x27;pending&#x27;. Once the campaign ends, we run a final view verification check and release the funds to your Vyro wallet&#x27;s available balance, which is ready to cash out. You can withdraw once you&#x27;ve earned a minimum of $10, once per week. Withdrawals are processed via Stripe or PayPal depending on your location.</div></div></div><div data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical"><h3 data-orientation="vertical" data-state="closed" class="flex"><button type="button" aria-expanded="false" data-state="closed" data-orientation="vertical" id="radix-_R_3pslnebsivb_" class="group flex flex-1 cursor-pointer items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left outline-hidden transition-all text-content-primary py-5 text-base font-medium md:text-lg" data-radix-collection-item=""><span class="text-lg font-medium">Why was my post rejected?</span><div class="bg-brand-primary/10 text-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:bg-brand-primary group-data-[state=open]:text-surface-primary rounded-full p-3"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1em" height="1em" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 256 256" class="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 transition-transform duration-200 group-data-[state=open]:rotate-45" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M228,128a12,12,0,0,1-12,12H140v76a12,12,0,0,1-24,0V140H40a12,12,0,0,1,0-24h76V40a12,12,0,0,1,24,0v76h76A12,12,0,0,1,228,128Z"></path></svg></div></button></h3><div data-state="closed" id="radix-_R_7pslnebsivb_" role="region" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_3pslnebsivb_" data-orientation="vertical" class="data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-reveal overflow-hidden data-[state=closed]:hidden motion-reduce:animate-none text-content-primary/70 text-sm leading-relaxed md:text-base" style="--radix-accordion-content-height:var(--radix-collapsible-content-height);--radix-accordion-content-width:var(--radix-collapsible-content-width)"><div class="pb-5">Posts are reviewed against the rules listed in the campaign brief and platform standards. The most common reasons are not following the campaign brief, missing the required ad disclosure, or low quality and spammy content. If your post is flagged for fraudulent or botted views, your account may be permanently banned from Vyro. If you think your post was rejected in error, you can reach out to our support team.</div></div></div></div><a href="https://app.vyro.com/support" data-slot="button" class="inline-flex transition-all
